Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


<div id='Model'></div>

# Model [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FarSky，一种结合任务感知潜空间耦合与潜在扩散模型的太阳辐照度概率预测框架，在确定性、概率预测及爬坡事件检测上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法预测精度高但多为确定性输出，难以捕捉爬坡事件，缺乏概率信息。

Method: 利用多任务自编码器学习图像重建与辐照度估计的共享潜空间，再以潜在扩散模型生成未来潜状态并解码为辐照度预测，通过随机采样获得概率分布。

Result: 在西班牙两个独立测试集上，确定性/概率预测技能最高提升11个百分点，爬坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 生成模型结合任务感知潜空间耦合能显著提升太阳辐照度预测性能，尤其适用于爬坡事件场景。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [158] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 商业AI检测器无法区分AI编辑与完整LLM草稿，导致合规的AI辅助编辑被高比例误判，而人类化改写可近乎完全逃避检测，检测分数不应作为学术不端的独立证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校依赖商业AI检测器维护学术诚信，但这些工具无法区分AI编辑与全文生成，可能将两类行为均视为不端。研究旨在量化这一政策失效的程度，并揭示检测分数的偏差来源。

Method: 对已发表英文摘要（四个领域，2013–2015 vs. 2023–2025）进行受控研究，以tau=0.50的代理人工/AI标签，测试轻量'仅润色摘要'（符合指南的AI辅助代理）与未修改原文在Pangram/GPTZero上的标记率，并分析Undetectable AI人类化后的检测率及分数与长词元、学术词汇密度的关联。

Result: 轻量编辑被标记率为64–80%；未修改2023–2025原文被标记率9–15%，非STEM显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高分数与长词元及学术词汇密度相关而非作者意图。人类化后检测率<4%，FNR>96%。

Conclusion: 诚实的AI编辑面临比人类化规避更高的处罚风险，检测分数不应单独作为学术不端证据。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [159] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出决策-资源视角下的终端对称性，分解为传输-刷新-验证框架，并实例化为方法，在多个任务上显著提升效率与验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构建任务在完成时具有精确对称性，但执行过程具有方向性和历史依赖性，现有方法未充分利用这一终端对称性作为决策资源。

Method: 通过过程证据提供方向性、终端对应传输结构、实现状态刷新决策相关性、固定验证器认证执行，形成传输-刷新-验证机制，并实例化为具有固定传输过程结构、状态限制过程秩、状态依赖残差秩及序数秩交集的特定算法。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充任务中，状态级刷新分别将AUC提升6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点；在1135个目标移除场景中，以最低平均验证成本超越对比方法。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可作为一种可复用的决策资源，显著提升定向构建任务的效率与验证性能。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [160] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Distribird是一个智能体网络应用，可自动从文献中为基于过程的模型参数构建信息性先验分布，通过多智能体流程搜索、加权并拟合分布，并回退到无信息备选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建信息性先验通常需要领域和统计双重专业知识，导致研究人员几乎总是使用均匀先验，这限制了贝叶斯校准的质量。

Method: 给定参数名、物理描述和领域背景，Distribird通过多智能体流程检索文献、按领域相关性提取和加权报告值，并用AIC模型选择拟合概率分布；无文献时回退到无信息备选方案，并报告证据和置信度。

Result: 在24个参数、10个科学领域的评估中，完整流程的先验质量与单提示LLM基线相当；但提供了可追溯来源、内置有效性层拒绝越界请求（基线在11/30情况下给出无依据先验），且所有语言模型调用均在本地运行。

Conclusion: 对于科学用途，可追溯性、有效性和隐私性比点估计精度的边际提升更重要，这些属性使Distribird更适合实际科学应用。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [161] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种市场信息感知的适配框架，基于Chronos-2时序基础模型实现日前电价预测的跨市场迁移，仅更新1%参数，在四个中国省级市场将MAE/RMSE平均降低6.24%/7.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但现有监督方法依赖目标市场历史数据，难以应用于新建或数据稀缺市场，需要有效的迁移方案。

Method: 构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口对齐7天价格上下文与预清算供需、备用、检修、机组容量和联络线变量；训练源域门控低秩适配器（LoRA），门控根据备用紧张度和运行状态信号缩放冻结的源适配器；采用留一市场交叉验证评估。

Result: 在四个中国省级日前现货市场上，相对零样本Chronos-2 MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%，相对普通Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%；实验排除全局标量和随机门初始化的替代解释，且相对Source-LoRA的额外提升有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入和状态依赖门控LoRA为数据稀缺电力市场提供了实际可行的迁移路径，但改进主要源于MSMI接口，门控机制贡献相对有限。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [162] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SAPO，一种将提示词分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式的段级自动提示优化方法，通过针对性强弱样本分析实现局部改进，在多个基准上超越现有APO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动提示优化（APO）方法通常整体重写提示词，容易导致改进某一行为的同时损害其他性能，缺乏对提示词内部结构的细粒度优化。

Method: SAPO将提示词分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式四个段，利用top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性分析；优化循环采用单一LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出，完成分段、弱点分析和候选生成；包含训练/验证协议和两阶段生成过程：（1）段级诊断与推荐提取，（2）受强弱段信号约束的候选合成。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini评估，SAPO的平均得分优于Zero-shot以及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA和StraGO等强基线。

Conclusion: 段级分解与针对性优化能有效避免整体重写的副作用，提升提示词优化的精度和鲁棒性，为APO提供了一种更可控的替代方案。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [163] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出P&ID Pilot，一个端到端AI流水线，结合遗传算法与大型语言模型，实现从工艺流程图的自动生成到管道仪表图的自动化转换，并验证了其有效性与领域合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前工艺流程图和管道仪表图的创建及转换主要依赖人工，耗时且成本高；引入AI可望实现自动化、节省时间，并通过探索多种拓扑方案降低人工劳动和财务成本。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：第一阶段用混合方法（遗传算法+大型语言模型）合成最优且有效的PFD拓扑；第二阶段用基于大型语言模型的智能体，通过受限工程软件开发工具包生成并执行经验证的修改，将PFD转换为P&ID。

Result: 混合方法生成最优PFD，损失值最低且满足出口流量参数和工程规则；LLM智能体实现100%执行成功率，同时保持领域规则和参考图结构合规。

Conclusion: 该统一流水线为端到端工艺设计自动化提供了可行路径，生成有效且可部署的输出，大幅减少人工工程工作量。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [164] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种结合UCB与重优化技术的上下文背包赌博机算法，将平均遗憾从O(1/√T)降至O((ln T)^3/T)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重优化的动态定价算法遗憾界为O(1/√T)，本文旨在利用问题结构（线性奖励、有限类型）实现更紧的遗憾界，提升学习效率。

Method: 将UCB算法扩展至上下文背包赌博机，每期基于置信上界估计奖励并采用重优化方法在线求解资源约束下的分配策略。

Result: 在T长度内达到平均遗憾O((ln T)^3/T)，显著优于文献中O(1/√T)的界。

Conclusion: 重优化+UCB能有效利用线性结构与约束，实现近乎最优的在线学习性能，为相关动态决策问题提供了更优理论保证。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [165] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出基于引导离散扩散的模型无关组合优化框架，用于解决品类优化问题，无需显式枚举即可生成高质量且多样化的品类集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统参数化选择模型（如MNL）在模型误设下性能脆弱，难以捕捉复杂顾客行为，且组合枚举在高维场景下不可行。

Method: 将品类表示为二元向量，通过学得的反向扩散过程进行随机搜索；引入奖励引导机制，用期望收益估计偏置局部转移，平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在经验测试中一致识别出高质量品类，对模型误设鲁棒，高维下常恢复近优解，且能生成多样化的高表现品类。

Conclusion: 生成式扩散模型为数据驱动决策中的组合优化提供了可扩展且鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [166] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Click2Poly 是一个基于 Florence-2 视觉语言模型、以人类为中心交互的 QGIS 插件，通过点击直接编辑建筑和墙体矢量图层，以加速制图质检与人工修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑和墙体的精确矢量制图对地理空间应用至关重要，但现有自动化提取方法仍难以达到制图标准，需要大量人工进行质量控制和复杂案例修正，过程耗时费力。

Method: 扩展 Florence-2 视觉语言模型，将其适配为响应点击输入来直接修改或编辑矢量图层；集成到 QGIS 插件中，实现人机协同的交互式编辑。

Result: 在真实生产环境中，该插件有效加快了建筑与墙体矢量图层的人工编辑速度，验证了其作为实用 AI 辅助工具的效率。

Conclusion: Click2Poly 展示了将大型视觉语言模型融入专业 GIS 工具以简化人工标注环节的潜力，为自动化地理数据提取后处理提供了一种可行的人机协同方案。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [167] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出边基连续p-中位问题，用两种模型划分道路网络为紧凑连通区域，并验证了基于最短路径约束的模型在计算效率上显著优于割集约束的B&C方法，且该约束可作为超有效不等式，同时揭示与边基区域划分问题的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区域划分模型在处理大规模道路网络时，因连通性约束的复杂性而难以求解，亟需更高效的建模与算法。

Method: 提出两种二值规划模型：割集约束模型（配合B&C分离算法）和最短路径连通性约束模型（可直接用商业求解器），并分析后者作为超有效不等式的性质。

Result: 在超2700节点、近3400边的网络上，SPC模型求解速度最高提升17倍；割集模型在EBD问题中12小时内无法找到可行解，而SPC模型可求解大部分实例至最优。

Conclusion: 基于最短路径的连通性约束在计算效率和可行性上均优于割集约束，且与EBD问题存在结构联系，为大规模道路网络分区提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [168] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 该论文证明在特定布尔Min-IP任务上，核注意力与完全注意力存在指数级表达能力差距，且该差距随上下文长度增长而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未充分揭示核注意力因压缩序列为固定维度草图而丧失的细粒度信息，尤其在处理包含竞争候选的短序列时，其能力边界不明确。

Method: 通过构造Min-IP布尔输入任务，对比单头归一化非负核注意力与密集softmax注意力的最坏情况性能，并利用信息论和组合论证推导特征数下界；同时针对确定性多层模型，基于交叉通道有限字母表建立转录下界。

Result: 核注意力需$2^{Ω(m)}$个特征才能在三token序列上误差低于1/2，而softmax仅需$m$维分数和常数温度；下界随上下文长度逼近精确$2^m$特征实现，并扩展至位置相关映射和因果查询；多核头模型在有限字母表下的转录下界与独立答案数线性相关。

Conclusion: 核注意力在短序列上无法匹敌完全注意力的表达能力，且差距呈指数级，表明当前高效注意力机制可能牺牲关键能力，需重新评估其适用场景。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [169] [Geometry-aware Incremental Neural Operator for Long-Horizon PDE prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11237)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Haifan Meng,Yi Lu,Zhihan Lyu,Fan Mo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出几何感知增量神经算子GeoIncNO，通过低秩投影约束活跃频带通道耦合、均值-波动解耦重建与相位校正，提升长时间PDE预测的稳定性与谱保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子长期自回归预测中，局部误差累积导致谱不一致、相位错位或均值漂移；已有方法改进状态表示与骨干网络，但未充分约束反复应用的潜在增量转变，使谱误差与不稳定通道耦合在展开中累积。

Method: GeoIncNO预测残差潜增量，利用基于增量谱能量分布确定的活跃频带上的轻量低秩投影器调节通道耦合；引入均值-波动解耦重建机制，分别融合稳定均值结构与动态波动，仅对零均值波动分量施加相位校正。

Result: 在六个涵盖1D/2D/3D动力系统的PDE基准上，GeoIncNO相比竞争性神经算子基线，取得更优预测精度、更稳定的长期展开及更好的谱保真度。

Conclusion: 通过显式结构化潜在增量与物理空间解耦重建，GeoIncNO有效缓解误差累积，为长时程PDE预测提供了稳定且谱可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Neural operators have shown strong potential for learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, long-horizon autoregressive prediction remains challenging: local errors accumulate as spectral inconsistency, phase misalignment, or mean drift. Existing methods mainly improve state representations and operator backbones, while leaving the repeatedly applied latent transition increment weakly structured, allowing spectral errors and unstable channel couplings to accumulate during rollout. To address these issues, we propose a geometry-aware incremental neural operator (GeoIncNO) for stable long-horizon PDE prediction. GeoIncNO predicts latent increments for residual advancement and uses lightweight low-rank projectors to regulate channel coupling within active frequency bands derived from the increment spectral energy distribution. To reduce physical-space reconstruction errors, GeoIncNO further introduces a mean--fluctuation decoupled reconstruction mechanism, where stable mean structures and dynamic fluctuations are fused separately, and phase correction is applied only to the zero-mean fluctuation component. Extensive experiments on six PDE benchmarks, covering 1D, 2D, and 3D dynamical systems, show that GeoIncNO achieves consistently strong prediction accuracy, improved rollout stability, and better spectral fidelity compared with competitive neural-operator baselines.

</details>


### [170] [How China-Origin Vision-Language Models Move from Refusal to Reframing in State Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11816)
*Guang Yang,Fengchen Liu,Alex Wang,Homa Hosseinmardi,Amir Ghasemian*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 首个系统评估中国多模态大模型政治审查的研究，发现审查从显性拒答转向隐性改写，且中文提示提升3倍改写概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦文本模型的审查，缺乏对多模态系统是否及如何实现国家立场对齐的系统性检验。

Method: 构建200条敏感话题基准和七种视觉抽象探针，用四种提示范式、两种语言测试9个VLM，通过双评审LLM在六维度独立审计。

Result: 中文提示将改写几率提升约3倍；中国模型改写量是外国模型的1.6\~3.2倍；文本评论中改写率最高（36.5%）；Qwen系列中改写上升而拒绝下降。

Conclusion: 审查从显性拒绝迁移至隐性重述，消除用户识别信息被扣留的线索，构成人机交互层面的深层问题。

Abstract: State-aligned distortion has been documented in China-origin text-based large language models (LLMs), but whether, and in what form, it arises in multimodal systems has not been systematically examined. We construct a balanced benchmark of 200 core entries spanning ten politically sensitive topics, plus a seven-variant visual-abstraction probe, and run nine vision-language models (VLMs), seven China-origin and two non-China, across four elicitation paradigms and two prompt languages, yielding 21,708 trials. Each response is audited on six dimensions -- explicit refusal, information integrity, visual grounding, state-aligned framing, language consistency, and response length -- by two independent frontier LLM judges, validated against three human experts on a 200-trial sample. Measuring each dimension separately lets us decompose multimodal censorship into individual signals rather than a single refusal-based score; in particular, refusal and framing are measured independently, so a model can stop refusing while still reframing. We find that (i) Chinese-language prompting roughly triples the odds of state-aligned framing, within every model; (ii) China-origin models reframe more than non-China models (direction robust across judges and human raters; magnitude 1.6--3.2x); (iii) the effect is strongest in text-only political commentary (36.5%) and is gated by recognition of the depicted subject rather than pixel detail, persisting even at silhouette for iconic images; and (iv) across four Qwen generations, state-aligned framing rises while explicit refusal falls: censorship migrates from a visible act (refusal) to an invisible one (fluent reframing). We argue this shift to invisible reframing is fundamentally a problem of human-AI interaction: it removes the very signal users rely on to recognize that information has been withheld.

</details>


### [171] [VQ-bench: A Composable Vector Quantization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11240)
*Ashwin Padaki,Amir Ingber,Edo Liberty*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架以组合7种量化原语，重表达25种常见量化器，并开源VQ-bench以推进量化算法研发与基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化虽为老问题，但因AI基础设施需求激增而重现活力，现有方法缺乏统一描述与公平比较平台，制约了算法创新与工程落地。

Method: 定义7个通用概念性量化原语，支持任意组合构建流水线；将25种现有量化器重新表达为这些原语的管道，并提供可扩展的开源基准框架VQ-bench。

Result: 实现了对多种量化算法的统一建模与可复现基准测试，验证了原语组合的表达力，并公开代码以支持社区扩展与对比。

Conclusion: 该框架和基准有助于系统化设计、评估和比较量化方案，推动AI基础设施中向量量化技术的快速迭代与标准化。

Abstract: Vector quantization is an old problem but has recently become central to AI infrastructure. It is therefore experiencing a surge of renewed engineering and research activity. This paper provides a unified framework for developing and benchmarking new quantization algorithms. We describe 7 common conceptual quantization primitives and show how to compose them arbitrarily. We then re-express 25 common quantizers as pipelines of these primitives. Finally, we publish VQ-bench as open-source to be extended further and make reproducible benchmarks publicly available.

</details>


### [172] [A Comparison of Malware Image Transformations Using Grad-CAM and Hybrid Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12077)
*Vibha Bhavikatti,Mark Stamp*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究将Grad-CAM作为可解释AI工具，分析八种恶意软件图像转换技术，发现解释忠实度与分类精度并不一致，并利用Grad-CAM特征训练随机森林模型，在17个恶意软件家族上达到0.777的测试准确率，超过先前基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制转图像方法在恶意软件检测中性能差异大，且图像模型的解释性与可解释性研究不足，需系统评估XAI工具的有效性。

Method: 使用Grad-CAM生成热力图，量化其忠实性与稳定性指标，并与HiResCAM对比；基于MobileNetV2提取Grad-CAM图像特征，训练随机森林分类器。

Result: 随机森林模型在17个恶意软件家族上测试准确率为0.777，优于先前基准0.750；同时发现不同图像转换技术的精度与解释忠实度不重合。

Conclusion: Grad-CAM热力图能为恶意软件分类提供有用特征，但高精度的模型未必产生最忠实的解释，需权衡二者选择图像转换技术。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that binary-to-image representations can enable effective machine learning-based results for malware detection and classification. However, performance can vary significantly, depending on the technique used to convert binaries to images. Furthermore, the explainability and interpretability of image-based models is largely unexplored within the malware domain. In this research, we employ Gradient-weighted Class Activation Maps (Grad-CAM) as an eXplainable AI (XAI) tool, which we use to analyze eight distinct image types derived from malware samples. We provide quantitative faithfulness and stability metrics for Grad-CAM heatmaps and we compare these heatmaps to High-Resolution Class Activation Mappings (HiResCAM). We also show that Grad-CAM heatmaps can provide useful information for malware classification. Specifically, we show that a Random Forest model trained on features extracted from Grad-CAM images via a MobileNetV2 Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) model achieves a test accuracy of 0.777 across 17 malware families, exceeding a previous benchmark of 0.750 for this same dataset. A key finding of this research is that for the malware image transformations considered, accuracy and explanation faithfulness do not coincide, e.g., image transformation techniques that produce the most faithful explanations yield only mid-tier accuracy.

</details>


### [173] [RelShap: Relationally Consistent Shapley Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11508)
*Seungeun Lee,Joao Fonseca,Julia Stoyanovich*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出RelShap框架，将关系数据库的完整性约束和数据溯源融入Shapley值计算，限制背景数据与联盟评估为关系合法配置，在保持估计器性质的同时提升解释忠实度，并通过函数依赖加速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习流水线常将关系数据扁平化为单表，丢失结构约束。传统Shapley值基于特征独立性假设，可能评估现实中不存在的组合，导致误导性解释。

Method: RelShap通过约束背景数据和联盟评估为关系合法配置，兼容Kernel SHAP、Monte Carlo和Leverage SHAP。利用函数依赖诱导联盟等价类，在不改变Shapley值的前提下减少运行时间，并提供组合学上的加速期望刻画。

Result: 多数据集、多模型和多估计器实验表明，RelShap解释更忠实于数据生成过程，在受控实验中能正确识别主导特征，而Conditional SHAP与ManifoldShap等方法失败。

Conclusion: RelShap提供了一种结合关系约束的稳健特征归因方法，兼顾解释正确性与计算效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine learning pipelines commonly flatten relational data into single-table representations, discarding structural constraints. Widely used Shapley value-based feature attributions then rely on feature independence, evaluating the model on combinations that could never arise in the underlying data, producing misleading explanations. We propose RelShap, a framework that incorporates relational constraints and data provenance into Shapley value computation, restricting both background data and coalition evaluation to relationally valid configurations. The framework is estimator-agnostic and composes with Kernel SHAP, Monte Carlo, and Leverage SHAP without altering their sampling or weighting properties. Functional dependencies further induce equivalence classes over feature coalitions, which RelShap exploits to reduce runtime without changing Shapley values; we provide a combinatorial characterization of the expected speedup. Experiments across multiple datasets, models, and estimators show that RelShap produces explanations that are more faithful to the data-generating process, correctly identifying the dominant feature in controlled settings where existing methods, including Conditional SHAP and ManifoldShap, do not. Our code is available at: https://github.com/duneag2/relshap.

</details>


### [174] [Towards the Harness of Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11246)
*Qi Wang,Tianyi Wang,Chengyang Li,Shikun Ban,Yurun Chen,Yizhong Ge,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Thea框架，将编码智能体的成功范式扩展到物理世界具身智能体，通过场景图上下文和评估即退出码机制，弥合物理世界与软件环境的状态读取和结果判断差距，实现长时程任务完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的成功证明了外围基础设施（harness）对智能体性能的决定性作用，而物理世界中的具身智能体是否同样受益于这种范式尚不清楚。关键挑战在于物理世界无法像软件环境那样自由读取全局状态和即时判断动作结果。

Method: Thea采用智能体循环编排机器人能力（封装为可调用工具），并引入两个核心机制：1）场景图作为上下文——维护持久化、符号化的世界状态表示；2）评估作为退出码——检测动作终止条件，判断成功与否，失败时诊断原因。二者形成闭环连接智能体与物理世界。

Result: 丰富的行为从工具组合中涌现，闭环机制支撑在真实环境中完成长时程（long-horizon）任务，验证了harness范式在具身智能领域的适用性。

Conclusion: 物理世界可通过适配的harness设计（场景图+评估机制）弥补其与软件环境的差距，使具身智能体真正受益于编码智能体已验证的工程化范式，为构建可靠物理世界智能系统提供新路径。

Abstract: The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

</details>


### [175] [FLARE++: Low-rank attention with dynamic attention routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11519)
*Vedant Puri,Yongjie Jessica Zhang,Levent Burak Kara*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: FLARE++ 是一种动态令牌路由的低秩注意力架构，通过输入条件化查询替代固定查询，在保持线性复杂度的同时提升 PDE 代理和长序列任务的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定低秩注意力模型（如 FLARE）使用训练后不变的查询模板，无法适应不同输入，限制了表达能力和泛化性。

Method: 复用 FLARE 的编码器，用少量学习的潜在种子（seeds）额外编码一次，将 N 个输入令牌聚合成 M 个输入相关的查询，再用这些查询压缩和重分配令牌，全程仅使用标准缩放点积注意力（SDPA），并支持多 GPU 上下文并行分片。

Result: 在多个 PDE 基准上比 FLARE 平均提升 24%，在 Long Range Arena 上平均准确率提高 2.3 点。

Conclusion: 动态输入条件化查询能显著增强低秩注意力模型的适应能力，且不牺牲效率，适用于高分辨率不规则域问题。

Abstract: Full self-attention is a strong token mixer for PDE surrogates on irregular domains, but its quadratic cost limits its use on high-resolution problems. Efficient latent-attention models such as the Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE) avoid that cost by routing all N tokens through M << N learned latent queries, but those queries are parameters: once trained, the same learned query templates serve every input. We remove this restriction with FLARE++, a low-rank attention architecture with dynamic token routing. FLARE++ reuses FLARE's own encoder to build its routing queries: learned latent seeds drive one extra encode call that gathers the N input tokens into M input-conditioned queries, and those queries then determine how the same tokens are compressed and redistributed. This preserves FLARE's explicit low-rank factorization and linear O(NM) complexity, and expresses the complete routing operation with standard scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) calls alone. We also provide a multi-GPU context-parallel implementation that shards input tokens across devices without ever gathering the full token sequence on one of them. FLARE++ is competitive across a set of standard PDE surrogate benchmarks, improving on fixed-query FLARE by 24% on average, and it gains 2.3 points of average accuracy on Long Range Arena.

</details>


### [176] [Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning in Digital Twin-Based Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11532)
*Qasim Zia,Saide Zhu,Haoxin Wang,Zafar Iqbal,Yingshu Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对DT-VANET中数据异质性和稀疏性导致联邦学习模型精度低的问题，提出层级联邦迁移学习框架，结合车辆类型聚类与数据质量评分机制，提升全局模型准确性并抵御恶意车辆攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习在车辆数据异构和稀疏场景下难以训练出高精度全局模型，且缺乏对恶意车辆的防护，影响数字孪生车联网的预测准确性。

Method: 构建DT-VANET框架，将联邦迁移学习与车辆类型聚类结合，提出层级联邦迁移学习（HFTL），设计云服务器模型更新与簇内联邦迁移学习两种算法，并引入基于数据质量评分的防御机制。

Result: 在真实数据集上通过多项性能指标验证了算法的有效性和效率，显著提升了全局模型精度。

Conclusion: HFTL有效解决了数据异质性和稀疏性问题，同时增强了模型安全性，为DT-VANET提供了一种鲁棒的联邦学习方案。

Abstract: In recent research on the Digital Twin-based Vehicular Ad hoc Network(DT-VANET), Federated Learning (FL) has shown its ability to provide data privacy. However, Federated learning struggles to adequately train a global model when confronted with data heterogeneity and data sparsity among vehicles, which ensure suboptimal accuracy in making precise predictions for different vehicle types. To address these challenges, this paper combines Federated Transfer Learning (FTL) to conduct vehicle clustering related to types of vehicles and proposes a novel Hierarchical Federated Transfer Learning (HFTL). We construct a framework for DT-VANET, along with two algorithms designed for cloud server model updates and intra-cluster federated transfer learning, to improve the accuracy of the global model. In addition, we developed a data quality score-based mechanism to prevent the global model from being affected by malicious vehicles. Lastly, detailed experiments on real-world datasets are conducted, considering different performance metrics that verify the effectiveness and efficiency of our algorithm.

</details>


### [177] [Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) From Model- and Feature-Space Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11541)
*Manya Singh,Mark T. Keane,Arjun Pakrashi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出RAD框架，通过模型空间一致性和特征空间一致性双指标量化预测模糊性，并用Pareto排名实现主动弃权，达到与现有拒绝方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的模型难以识别预测模糊性，而高 stakes 场景需要模型对模糊预测弃权或标记人工检查。

Method: 定义两个互补指标：模型空间一致性（评估功能等价模型间的预测稳定性）和特征空间一致性（评估输入微扰下的预测稳定性），结合为RAD得分对，并用RAD绘图可视化；下游用Pareto排名筛选高模糊样本弃权。

Result: 在受控重叠的合成数据和多组真实数据上验证，弃权策略性能与现有基线相当。

Conclusion: RAD提供可解释的模糊性量化工具，有效支持高置信决策场景中的弃权或人工干预。

Abstract: Machine learning models should be robust, in the sense of remaining predictively consistent under permissible variations. A model's predictions should ideally remain unchanged when it is replaced by a functionally equivalent one, or when its inputs are subject to minor, admissible perturbations. If such changes alter a prediction significantly, then the prediction is "ambiguous" with respect to the model. Models should abstain from making such ambiguous predictions and/or should flag them for human inspection, especially in high-stakes decision-making scenarios. However, in practice, such ambiguity is not easy to identify once a model is deployed. Here, the Robust Ambiguity Detection (RAD) framework is advanced for quantifying predictive ambiguity using two complementary metrics: Model-Space Consistency and Feature-Space Consistency. These two scores, the RAD Score-Pair, visualised through the RAD Plot, provide an interpretable characterisation of the sources of ambiguity and the actions a user may consider in response. RAD is evaluated on synthetic datasets with systematically controlled overlap, as well as several real-world datasets where the level of ambiguity cannot be directly inspected. Finally, we demonstrate a downstream application of RAD where samples are ranked by their RAD Pareto-Rank and the most ambiguous are abstained from prediction, achieving performance comparable to existing rejection-based approaches.

</details>


### [178] [Symbolic Machine Learning for Vapor-Liquid Equilibrium Prediction in Cx-N2 Binary Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11255)
*Bongseok Kim,Suman Chakraborty,Gary Huang,Mehek Mathur,Guang Lin,Li Qiao*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种符号机器学习方法，为Peng-Robinson状态方程发现可解释的符号修正项，显著提高烃-氮混合物气液平衡预测精度，并支持跨碳数外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方状态方程对烃-氮混合物的VLE预测在宽组成和链长范围内不准确，深度学习方法虽准确但缺乏可解释性和解析表达式，需要一种兼顾精度与透明度的修正方案。

Method: 采用两级策略：先对单个烃系统识别符号表达式，再将其系数表示为碳数的函数，从而适配不同碳数的烃系统。

Result: 在所有研究的烃-氮系统中，预测精度相对原始PR-EOS均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为改进PR-EOS对烃-氮VLE的预测提供了可解释的符号修正途径。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of vapor--liquid equilibrium (VLE) for hydrocarbon-nitrogen mixtures remains challenging for cubic equations of state, particularly across broad ranges of composition and hydrocarbon chain length. While deep learning models can provide accurate predictions, they often lack interpretability and explicit analytical expressions. In this work, we propose a symbolic machine learning approach to discover interpretable symbolic corrections to Peng-Robinson equation-of-state (PR-EOS) predictions from experimental data. The proposed approach adopts a two-level strategy: symbolic expressions are first identified for individual hydrocarbon systems, after which their coefficients are represented as functions of carbon number to enable accurate prediction across different hydrocarbon systems. The results demonstrate significantly improved prediction accuracy over the original PR-EOS across all hydrocarbon-nitrogen systems. Overall, the proposed approach provides an interpretable symbolic correction framework for improving PR-EOS predictions of hydrocarbon-nitrogen VLE.

</details>


### [179] [Adaptive Hybrid Particle Swarm Optimization with Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11258)
*Aryan Gurudeo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出自适应混合粒子群优化（AHPSO），通过群体多样性sigmoid函数自动调节梯度注入强度，在探索阶段近零、开发阶段近最大，无需手动切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度注入仅在群体进入具有平滑局部结构的盆地时对PSO有帮助，并非普遍有效；现有方法缺乏对梯度影响的自适应调节机制和适用条件的明确刻画。

Method: AHPSO使用群体多样性作为输入，经sigmoid函数计算梯度影响权重，实现探索与开发阶段的平滑过渡；比较采用预算归一化（相同总函数评估次数）和迭代匹配两种协议。

Result: 预算归一化下PSO在40个配置中胜率52.5% vs AHPSO 20%（p=7.0e-5）；迭代匹配下29个函数、42配置、14,700次运行中AHPSO-Adadelta在9种方法（含CMA-ES）中排名第一（p=9.75e-4），优势集中在F8、F24-F27等平滑局部盆地问题。

Conclusion: 贡献在于原理性刻画梯度注入在群智能搜索中何时有价值，而非宣称普遍优越性；AHPSO仅在特定问题类型上以相等代价优于无向采样。

Abstract: Gradient injection helps Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) only when the swarm has identified a basin with smooth local structure, not universally. We propose Adaptive Hybrid PSO (AHPSO), which uses a sigmoid function on swarm diversity to automatically modulate gradient influence: near-zero during exploration, near-maximum during exploitation, with no manual phase-switching. Under budget-normalized comparison (PSO given equivalent total function evaluations), PSO wins 52.5% of 40 configurations versus AHPSO's 20% (p = 7.0e-5, Friedman). AHPSO retains advantage specifically on problems with smooth local basins (F8, F24-F27) where directed descent outperforms undirected sampling even at equal cost. Under iteration-matched comparison across 29 functions (42 configurations, 14,700 runs), AHPSO-Adadelta ranks first of 9 methods including CMA-ES (p = 9.75e-4). The contribution is a principled characterization of when gradient injection provides value in swarm-based search, not a claim of universal superiority.

</details>


### [180] [RECAST: A Machine-Learning Framework for Correction and Super-Resolution of Coarse-Grid PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11572)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: RECAST是一个机器学习框架，通过在粗网格数值时间步进中加入学习校正并重建细网格状态，在保持粗网格演化效率的同时恢复解精度，在多种一维PDE系统中将误差降低50-92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗网格数值求解器可大幅降低时间相关PDE模拟的计算成本，但欠分辨率会同时降低轨迹和空间保真度，现有方法难以兼顾效率与精度。

Method: RECAST结合了数值时间步进循环内的学习校正模块和从校正后的粗网格历史重建细网格状态的超分辨率模块，形成闭环训练与推理。

Result: 在六个一维PDE系统（涵盖输运、扩散、色散、反应和波动）中，使用8-16倍粗化和1000步闭环滚动测试，相对误差降低约50-92%，并对未见PDE参数具有泛化性；在5000步滚动中优于当代粗校正架构。

Conclusion: RECAST证明了学习和校正能力可支持更粗的PDE演化而不损失解保真度，为高维数值模拟的机器学习加速提供了概念验证路径。

Abstract: Coarse-grid numerical solvers can substantially reduce the computational cost of time-dependent PDE simulation, but under-resolution often degrades both the trajectory and the spatial fidelity of the solution. We introduce RECAST (Recurrent Error Correction And Super-resolution of coarse-grid Trajectories), a machine-learning framework designed to restore this lost accuracy while retaining coarse-grid evolution. RECAST combines learned correction within the numerical time-stepping loop with reconstruction of the corresponding fine-grid state from the corrected coarse history. We evaluate the framework on six one-dimensional PDE systems spanning transport, diffusion, dispersion, reaction, and wave dynamics, using spatial grids coarsened by factors of 8-16 and 1000-step closed-loop rollouts from unseen initial conditions. Across the test cases, RECAST remains closely aligned with the fine-grid reference solutions and reduces time-averaged relative error by approximately 50-92% compared with the corresponding uncorrected coarse-grid solvers. Additional tests show generalization to unseen PDE parameter values, while comparison with a contemporary coarse-correction architecture shows that RECAST achieves lower error and better long-horizon agreement with the fine-grid reference over 5000-step rollouts. These results demonstrate that the learned correction and reconstruction capabilities of RECAST can enable substantially coarser PDE evolution without the corresponding loss of solution fidelity, providing a proof-of-concept route toward machine-learning acceleration of higher-dimensional numerical simulations across science and engineering.

</details>


### [181] [Dion3: Full-Stack Orthogonal Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11612)
*Noah Amsel,Jack Zhang,Kwangjun Ahn,Ali Naeimi,Austin Feng,Berlin Chen,Tri Dao,John Langford*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Dion3通过算法、内核和通信三方面优化，将Muon优化器的计算和通信开销降低最多6倍，同时保持或提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器因立方复杂度的Newton-Schulz正交化步骤和权重分片时的通信开销，导致训练成本过高，削弱其在实际场景中的优势。

Method: 提出Gram Newton-Schulz算法降低FLOP成本，用CuteDSL内核利用对称性加速，采用megabatching减少通信，并仅正交化动量矩阵的部分行以进一步减少开销。

Result: Dion3在匹配或超越Muon损失表现的同时，将优化器单步耗时最多降低6倍，并优于同为压缩版的Dion。

Conclusion: Dion3是一种高效的Muon替代方案，可作为drop-in替换直接使用，兼顾性能与速度。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer incurs a significant overhead cost due to its cubic-time Newton-Schulz orthogonalization step. When weights are sharded, communication overhead compounds this computational cost, eroding the benefits of Muon in many settings. We present Dion3, a revision of Muon that targets this overhead at every level of the stack. Our Gram Newton-Schulz algorithm reduces the FLOP cost of orthogonalization, our CuteDSL kernels accelerate it by exploiting symmetry, and our megabatching strategy reduces communication overhead. Moreover, we propose a simple change to the update rule that cuts costs even further: selecting only a fraction of the momentum matrix's rows to orthogonalize at each step. This update rule improves on Dion (another "compressed" version of Muon), in both speed and performance. Overall, Dion3 matches or improves on the loss achieved by Muon but reduces optimizer step time by up to 6x. Dion3 is available via the dion package (https://github.com/microsoft/dion) as a drop-in replacement for Muon.

</details>


### [182] [A Local Sinkhorn Framework for Conditional Distribution Reconstruction of Multidimensional Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11613)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种局部Sinkhorn散度框架，用于多维随机场的条件分布重建，通过去偏Sinkhorn散度实现可微且计算高效的局部分布匹配目标，并建立泛化误差界以平衡近似偏差与统计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件分布重建方法在多维随机场中面临几何保真度、统计效率与计算可扩展性之间的权衡，精确局部最优传输计算代价过高，亟需可扩展且实用的替代方案。

Method: 利用去偏Sinkhorn散度构造可微的局部分布匹配损失，用于训练随机神经网络（SNNs）；理论分析显式刻画正则化参数对近似偏差与统计效率的影响，并给出泛化误差估计。

Result: 通过多个数值实验，与现有损失函数及机器学习不确定性量化框架对比，所提方法在重建精度与计算效率间取得有效平衡，并保持良好的多维可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架为条件分布重建提供可扩展的替代方案，在几何保真、统计效率与计算可扩展性之间实现实际折中，适用于不确定性量化与概率科学机器学习。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a local Sinkhorn divergence framework for conditional distribution reconstruction of multidimensional random fields. By utilizing the debiased Sinkhorn divergence, our proposed approach develops a differentiable and computationally efficient local distribution matching objective to train stochastic neural networks (SNNs). Furthermore, we establish theoretical generalization error estimates for our local Sinkhorn divergence framework, which explicitly characterizes the trade-off between approximation bias and statistical efficiency controlled by the regularization parameter and reveals how our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence loss function can be efficiently applied to learning multidimensional random field models. The proposed framework provides a scalable alternative to exact local optimal transport for conditional distribution reconstruction, offering a practical compromise between geometric fidelity, statistical efficiency, and computational scalability for uncertainty quantification and probabilistic scientific machine learning. Through various numerical examples, we compare our proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework with other loss functions to train SNNs and with other machine-learning-based uncertainty quantification frameworks, demonstrating that the proposed local Sinkhorn divergence framework achieves an effective balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency while maintaining good scalability for multidimensional stochastic systems.

</details>


### [183] [Apodex Discovery: Reality Benchmarks and Environments for Evaluating and Building Discoverative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11341)
*Brian Wang,Bin Feng,Xiaoman Pan,Chenyang An,Felix Liu,Tangqi Fang,Gongbo Sun,Lingfeng Shen,Ning Wang,Handuo Zhang,Feng Chen,Fuchao Yang,Xiang Wang,Jiacheng Lin,Siting Li,Zixuan Liu,Chi Han,Zhenhailong Wang,Kunlun Zhu,Lawrence Zhao,Yueqi Guo,Kailong Wen,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Lidong Bing,David Tan,Bo An,Heng Ji,Sheng Wang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出Apodex Discovery框架，通过重载求解器将AI评估从预定义基准转向可验证的真实世界发现任务，在AAV载体设计和药物重定位等任务上超越现有水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型擅长解决已明确规范的问题，但真实世界挑战往往不具备可执行或可验证的形式，需要从‘解决难题’转向‘发现并验证问题’。

Method: 构建重载求解器（基础模型+工具+控制策略），包含问题侦察流程（调研561个行业、筛选20个问题）、统一环境-任务-回合抽象（提供数据/工具/约束/反馈/轨迹记录与验证）、HDS6评估维度（工具、修复、替代、连贯性、证据、范围）。

Result: 在AAV载体设计中超过已发表SOTA 7%；在药物重定位与改良中，GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6-sol的平均归一化预测分数分别提升2.5和7.6分；消融实验证明TRACES接口可归因性能差异。

Conclusion: 该框架推动AI评估超越固定基准，走向以可验证调查为核心的真正发现过程，为复杂现实问题提供系统化解决方案。

Abstract: Apollo did not reach the Moon merely because its engineers could solve difficult equations. It succeeded by turning a distant ambition into a mission architecture of explicit objectives, simulation, verification, and repeated correction. AI now faces a similar transition: frontier models can solve difficult tasks once the problem, tools, and success criteria are specified, yet consequential real-world challenges rarely arrive in an executable or verifiable form.
  We introduce Apodex Discovery, a framework for building and evaluating discoverative AI through the heavy-duty solver, a system comprising a foundation model, harness, tools, and control policies that pursues extended, stateful, verifiable investigations. It has three core components. First, a problem-scouting process surveyed 561 industries across 16 sectors, assembled 423 high-value real-world problems, and selected 20 for the initial release. Second, a common environment-task-episode abstraction provides data, tools, constraints, feedback, trajectory recording, and verification of intermediate artifacts and final submissions. Third, HDS6 evaluates Tools, Repair, Alternatives, Coherence, Evidence, and Scope independently of final-task success.
  In AAV capsid design, Apodex surpassed the published state of the art by 7% across viability, tropism, structure prediction, and generative design. In drug repurposing and reformulation, a task-specific biomedical environment improved the mean normalized prediction score of GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6-sol by 2.5 and 7.6 points over the same closed-book backbone. Controlled ablations show that the fixed TRACES episode interface enables attribution of performance differences to specific solver components. Apodex Discovery moves AI evaluation beyond predefined benchmarks toward verifiable investigations aimed at genuine discovery.

</details>


### [184] [FM-LLM: A frequency-enhanced mixture-of-experts framework for adapting LLMs to time series forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11623)
*Rentao Gu,Yihang Ding,Junjie Li,Yi Ding,Weijing Sang,Xiaoli Huo,Xin Qin,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出FM-LLM，一种无需文本提示、基于频域增强的混合专家框架，用于将冻结的LLM适配到时间序列预测任务，在11个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的跨模态时间序列预测方法过度依赖文本提示进行模态对齐，计算开销大且未能充分利用时间序列固有的频谱动态信息。

Method: FM-LLM采用自回归框架，通过傅里叶分析网络（FAN）的频谱令牌对齐器将结构化谐波表示直接注入冻结LLM；不对称混合专家（MoE）解码器实现角色分离（共享专家重建全局周期骨架，路由专家专攻非周期残差）；并使用时间-频率混合损失函数联合优化。

Result: 在11个公开基准的78项评估指标中59项达到SOTA；相比最强自回归LLM基线，MSE平均提升5.3%，MAE平均提升5.6%，最大分别提升8.0%和8.4%；在10%少样本和零样本场景中保持优越性能。

Conclusion: FM-LLM验证了无需文本提示的频域增强方法可有效适配冻结LLM进行时间序列预测，兼具高性能、低开销和良好的迁移性。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have spurred cross-modal solutions for time-series forecasting. However, existing methods rely heavily on textual prompts for modality alignment-introducing nontrivial computational overhead and failing to leverage the rich spectral dynamics inherent in time-series data. To enable prompt-free, frequency-aware adaptation of frozen LLMs, we propose FM-LLM (Frequency-Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for adapting LLMs to Time Series Forecasting), an autoregressive framework grounded in constrained asymmetric coupling. A Fourier Analysis Network (FAN)-based spectral token aligner injects structured harmonic representations directly into the frozen LLM with numerical compatibility. An asymmetric Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decoder enforces role separation: shared experts with lightweight FAN layers reconstruct the global periodic backbone, while routed experts-restricted to standard FFNs-specialize in modeling non-periodic residual dynamics. A time-frequency hybrid loss function jointly optimizes temporal accuracy and spectral consistency, mitigating error accumulation during long-horizon autoregressive rollouts. Evaluated across eleven public benchmarks, FM-LLM achieves state-of-the-art performance on 59 out of 78 evaluation metrics. Compared to the strongest autoregressive LLM-based baseline, it delivers average improvements of 5.3% in MSE and 5.6% in MAE, with maximum gains reaching 8.0% for MSE and 8.4% for MAE. FM-LLM also demonstrates robust transferability, maintaining superior performance in 10% few-shot and zero-shot forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [185] [Can Frontier LLMs Match Natively Multimodal Embeddings? A Comparison on Hard-Negative Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11343)
*Archan Dutta,Vyanktesh Kanungo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究首次直接对比了原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与基于LLM的视觉排序方法在Flickr30k上的表现，发现GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6与Gemini Embedding 2性能相当，但多模态嵌入在低延迟场景下更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态检索与分类传统上依赖双编码器模型，而Gemini Embedding 2的发布引入了原生多模态嵌入，同时前沿LLM也展现出视觉理解能力，因此需要明确两者在零样本排序任务中的相对优劣。

Method: 在Flickr30k数据集上，对原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与LLM-based视觉排序方法（GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6）进行直接对比评估，并考虑嵌入预计算带来的延迟影响。

Result: GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6在排序性能上与Gemini Embedding 2持平；但若嵌入可预先计算，多模态嵌入更适合低延迟应用。

Conclusion: 多模态嵌入在性能上与前沿LLM相当，且更适合低延迟部署，为实际系统选型提供了依据。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

</details>


### [186] [Low-Interaction-Rank Learning: Unifying Multiplicative Dual-Encoder Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11661)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出乘法双编码器网络的统一理论框架，通过交互秩和交互谱分析其表达能力、样本复杂度与可辨识性，并证明白化可消除规范对称性、恢复可解释模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该架构在多个领域独立发展但缺乏统一设计准则，需回答交互模式数量、编码器归一化方式及适用条件等基础问题。

Method: 定义低交互秩函数类，以交互谱度量内在复杂度；分解逼近误差为谱截断项与编码器实现项；证明样本复杂度由两个编码器复杂度之和决定；指出编码器仅定义到线性规范对称性，归一化即规范固定，白化可固定交互模式至置换与符号。

Result: 合成核、算子学习与CLIP实验验证谱衰减率预测；白化重建真实模式；独立训练的CLIP模型间存在单一旋转，去除后暴露可解释概念轴。

Conclusion: 该框架为乘法双编码器提供设计准则与可辨识性解决方案，白化作为实用补救工具，提升模型可解释性。

Abstract: A multiplicative dual-encoder network computes a real-valued output for a pair of inputs as the inner product of their separate encodings. This architecture has been developed independently in operator learning, bipartite matching, contrastive vision-language models, retrieval, and other areas, yet no unified theory guides the basic design decisions: how many interaction modes to represent, how to normalize the encoders, and when the architecture should be avoided. We provide such a foundation by introducing the class of functions of low interaction rank, a class whose intrinsic complexity is measured by its interaction spectrum. Within this framework, approximation error decomposes into a spectral truncation term and an encoder-realization term; sample complexity is governed by the sum of the two encoder complexities rather than their product; and a usability criterion based on spectral decay determines when the architecture can succeed. The same framework exposes a central identifiability problem: the encoders are defined only up to a linear gauge symmetry that leaves the learned coordinates arbitrary. We show that normalization is gauge fixing and that whitening pins the interaction modes up to permutation and sign, thereby explaining the uninterpretability of contrastive dimensions and providing a constructive remedy. Experiments on synthetic kernels, operator learning, and CLIP models validate the theoretical predictions: spectral decay rates match the predicted scaling, whitening recovers the true modes, and independently trained CLIP models are related by a single rotation which, after removal by whitening, exposes interpretable concept axes. The code of this paper is provided at https://github.com/RS2002/Mul-Net .

</details>


### [187] [High-dimensional Multi-objective Bayesian Optimization with Learned Variable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11713)
*Hongyan Wang,Jiayu Huang,Haotian Zheng,Xin Gao,Chi Ding,Ying Liu,Xia Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ViaMOBO，一种基于决策变量交互分析的高维昂贵多目标贝叶斯优化框架，通过分解决策空间进行局部优化，在基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOBO方法因指数级采样复杂度难以处理高维决策空间，需要一种能识别变量独立/依赖关系并分解空间的通用框架。

Method: 利用变量交互分析模型判断决策空间是否可完全或部分划分，进而将原问题分解为若干子空间，并在各子空间内执行局部贝叶斯优化，无需强假设即可区分可分离、部分可分离和不可分离目标。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上，ViaMOBO在逼近高维昂贵多目标问题的Pareto前沿方面优于多个最新MOBO基线。

Conclusion: ViaMOBO为高维昂贵多目标优化提供了一种有效的通用解决方案，通过变量交互分析显著提升了优化性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is effective in identifying the Pareto fronts for expensive black-box problems. However, most current MOBO approaches are limited to low-dimensional decision space due to its exponential sampling complexity. This paper presents decision variable interaction analysis-based MOBO, ViaMOBO, a generic framework for expensive multi-objective problems with high-dimensional decision space. The key idea of ViaMOBO is that it utilizes a variable interaction analysis model to determine whether the decision space can be completely or partially divided, and then performs local Bayesian optimization in the divided decision subspaces. Through the variable analysis model, it can be derived whether the objectives in black-box problems are separable, partially separable, or non-separable based on the potential independent or interdependent relationships among decision variables without any strong assumptions. We compare ViaMOBO with the state-of-the-art MOBO methods on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate that ViaMOBO outperforms other related MOBO baselines in approximating the Pareto front of high-dimensional expensive multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [188] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出MOON方法，在谱-核范数几何下进行梯度操作，利用正交归一化更新提升多任务优化效率，并给出理论收敛保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法将参数展平为向量并在欧氏空间操作梯度，忽视了Transformer等现代架构的矩阵结构，导致欧氏空间梯度操作并非矩阵几何下的最陡下降方向，限制优化效率。

Method: 基于矩阵值参数的最陡下降理论，在谱-核范数几何下执行梯度操作，并将操作后的梯度正交归一化用于参数更新。

Result: 在光滑非凸目标下，确定性场景帕累托平稳性度量收敛率为O(T^{-1/2})，随机梯度场景为O(T^{-1/4})；多个基准实验表明MOON一致提升优化效率和最终多任务性能。

Conclusion: MOON通过利用矩阵几何结构，有效提升多任务优化性能，为MOO在矩阵参数场景下提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [189] [FrontierFinance: A Challenging Benchmark for Measuring Frontier Intelligence of Finance Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11683)
*Yuhao Zhang,O. Ozan Koyluoglu,Thejas Venkatesh,Richard Diehl Martinez,Vishank Bhatia,Arash Alidoust,Ashwin Paranjape*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出FrontierFinance，一个覆盖完整投资者工作流的220个专家级查询和11,543条带来源评分标准的开源基准，评估显示工具框架对性能影响大于模型本身，最佳系统得分56.0%，筛选发现与行业宏观是最难用例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准仅覆盖数据提取这一狭窄环节，模型已趋饱和，且基于参考的指标和通用LLM评分无法适应开放式长答案分析查询，缺乏端到端投资者工作流的评估基准。

Method: 构建包含六个关键用例（覆盖完整工作流）的220个专家级查询和11,543条带来源评分标准，在统一测试框架下仅使用公开数据，评估前沿模型和代理系统，并比较工具框架、成本与性能的关系。

Result: Samaya内部系统以56.0%领先（成本约为最强前沿模型Claude Fable 5的2.2倍更低），最佳开源模型Kimi K3达46.4%（成本比最佳专有模型低4.5倍），Screening & Discovery和Sector, Industry & Macro是最难用例（最佳仅33%和39%）。

Conclusion: FrontierFinance比现有公共金融基准更广更难，工具框架对质量和效率影响显著，开源模型接近专有模型性能且成本更低，数据集和评分代码已公开以推动研究。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed for professional investment research, yet no benchmark captures the complexity of the full investor workflow. Existing benchmarks mainly target financial data extraction, a narrow slice that current models have largely saturated, while reference-based metrics and generic LLM-as-a-judge scoring fall short on the open-ended, long-form answers that real analyst queries demand. We introduce FrontierFinance, a fully open benchmark of 220 expert-crafted queries and 11,543 source-attributed rubrics spanning six crucial use cases across the full investor workflow. FrontierFinance is both broader and harder than existing public finance benchmarks. Evaluating frontier models and agent systems under a common harness restricted to publicly available data, we find that the tool harness, not the model alone, strongly shapes quality and efficiency; that Samaya's in-house system leads at 56.0%, ahead of the strongest frontier model (Claude Fable 5, 49.2%) at roughly 2.2x lower cost; and that the best open-weight model (Kimi K3, 46.4%) nearly matches the best proprietary model at 4.5x lower cost. Screening & Discovery and Sector, Industry & Macro remain the hardest use cases across all systems, where even the best systems reach only 33% and 39%. We make the dataset and grading code publicly available.

</details>


### [190] [Air Quality Station Simulation via LSTM and Attention-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11839)
*Alexander Kostadinov,Petar O. Hristov,Dessislava Petrova-Antonova*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SATADL深度学习模型，用于在监测站故障期间模拟其PM10浓度测量，实现长达48小时的多步空气污染预测，性能优于多个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量监测站硬件故障或断电会导致数据缺失，影响空气质量分析和公共健康预警，因此需要一个能临时替代故障站的虚拟代理模型。

Method: SATADL是一种深度学习架构，能从数据不同方面提取复杂关系，通过多步预测输出未来多小时空气质量，并详细评估各组件设计。

Result: 在四个全球不同地区的监测站数据集上，模拟1-48小时PM10故障期，SATADL在不同预测窗口下均优于基线和已发表的深度学习模型，在决定系数和均方根误差上表现更好。

Conclusion: SATADL可作为可靠的虚拟替代站，在真实监测站失效时提供连续、准确的空气质量预测，保障数据完整性和分析质量。

Abstract: Poor air quality in urban areas is driven by a complex chain of processes and presents a significant public health concern. To better understand and control the mechanisms that determine air quality, cities deploy networks of measurement stations, and launch initiatives for collecting denser data about the concentration of pollutants in the atmosphere. Extracting insights from the stations relies on their reliable and uninterrupted operation. However, hardware is susceptible to faults and black- outs that may result in data unavailability, which affects the overall quality of analyses. In this paper, we present a deep-learning model, called SATADL, which can infer complex relations and output multiple-hour-ahead air-quality forecasts. The goal of the model is to simulate the mea- surements of an unresponsive station until its operation is restored. The architecture of the model, which allows it to extract information from different aspects of the data, is described in detail and a careful examination of all of its components is provided. We demonstrate the performance of SATADL on four sets of air quality stations from around the world, by using it to simulate the concentration of PM10 for periods of hypothetical failures of one of the measurement stations, lasting for as long as 48 hours. A selection of baseline and published deep learning models were trained and used as a benchmark. The results show that SATADL per- forms better across different prediction windows, for both coefficient of determination and root mean squared error, demonstrating its suitability as a virtual proxy station.

</details>


### [191] [Small-Scale Experiments: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11859)
*Nicholas Lourie,Kyunghyun Cho,Karen Ullrich,Sanae Lotfi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 小规模模型中的缩放定律因超参数敏感性而难以显现，但通过充分调优可复现；超参数敏感度随规模增大而降低，且损失面维度下降。结合新方法论，小规模实验可准确预测大模型的架构选择（如归一化层位置）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律承诺低成本实验，但六年来在小规模上（4M参数起）不可靠，导致研究者误以为必须使用大模型。本文旨在揭示其失效的真正原因是超参数敏感性，而非缩放定律本身不成立，并恢复小规模实验的预测能力。

Method: 通过消融基础缩放定律配方，对比超参数与其他因素的作用；分析超参数损失面随规模变化的维度；结合现有文献提出模型中心研究的新方法论，并在Transformer归一化位置问题上验证。

Result: 超参数调优比其他因素更重要；超参数敏感性随规模增大而消失，损失面维度降低；小规模实验成功复现大规模结论（预归一化优于后归一化）。

Conclusion: 只要正确理解并处理超参数敏感性，小规模实验可以兑现缩放定律的承诺，为模型研究提供低成本且可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Scaling laws promised cost-effective experiments; six years later, they have yet to fully deliver. Instead, researchers have found them unreliable at small scales (starting at 4M parameters) and concluded that sizable models cannot be avoided. We show this is not the case: the confounding factor is hyperparameters. Small models are highly sensitive, but hyperparameter sensitivity fades with scale. This small-scale sensitivity makes scaling laws easy to miss because they only emerge on the fully tuned frontier, and reaching that frontier requires an extensive search far beyond what most ever run. By ablating the basic scaling law recipe, we show well-tuned hyperparameters matter more than any other ingredient. Further, we reveal why those hyperparameters become easier to find: as scale increases, the hyperparameter loss surface becomes lower dimensional. Nevertheless while scaling laws exist in small models, extrapolation hits statistical limitations. A holistic approach is required. Synthesizing our insights with the recent literature, we develop a new methodology for model-centric research and demonstrate it on a question that once took the field years to settle: where to place normalization layers in the transformer architecture. From small-scale experiments, we recover the large scale result: pre-normalization works better as models grow in size. With the right tools and a better understanding, small-scale experiments can deliver on scaling laws' long-awaited promise.

</details>


### [192] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 针对硅双极光电晶体管工艺开发中，小样本层级结构数据下的增益预测问题，提出一种结合方差分解、前向预测、逆向搜索与数据质量评估的综合方法，并开源数据集与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟计量依赖大数据，而实际工艺开发中样品少、层级嵌套，且工艺调试耗时数月，需在流片前预测器件增益以节省成本和时间。

Method: 首先对增益进行方差分解，区分批次间与批次内变异；其次构建基于工艺参数的前向预测器，输出相对且含不确定性的信号；再次提供逆向搜索以匹配目标增益的工艺配方；最后设计面向批次、晶圆、芯片嵌套结构的多层级数据质量评估，并引入跨层级关联分数。

Result: 发现约一半增益方差存在于工艺批次之间，证明仅依赖工艺配方的预测存在固有上限；所提方法在真实工艺历史（13-14次流片）上给出有效预测与搜索支持。

Conclusion: 该研究为小样本、层级化的半导体工艺建模提供了实用框架，强调数据质量与不确定性量化，且通过开源促进复现与进一步应用。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [193] [OEIS Open: How many conjectures can language models turn into theorems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11941)
*Tom Adamczewski*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于OEIS中492个开放数学猜想的基准OEIS Open，并发现配备最小工具集的通用语言模型能以每次50美元的预算解决其中147个猜想（得分30%），而增加arXiv文献或更复杂的代理循环并未提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往针对这些猜想仅使用定制代理进行尝试，缺乏可复现、可扩展且能防止作弊的通用评估框架，因此需要构建一个开源基准来公平评估任意语言模型在开放数学猜想上的自主推理能力。

Method: 选取OEIS中492个已被Lean形式化的开放猜想，编写开源评测代码，对任意通用语言模型提供最小工具集（如计算、查询等），并设置固定预算（每次50或200美元），同时评估加入arXiv文献和不同代理循环策略的影响。

Result: 最佳模型在50美元预算下解决147个猜想（30%）；在200美元预算下于OEIS Open Lite（100个随机子集）上得分44%；加入476,000篇arXiv论文或更复杂的代理循环未带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 语言模型能够以较低成本自主解决部分开放研究猜想，但额外文献和复杂代理策略并非必要；这些猜想数学意义不确定且少受关注，但结果仍展示了LM在数学发现领域的潜力。

Abstract: We construct OEIS Open, a benchmark based on 492 open mathematical conjectures from the OEIS, formalized in Lean by Tsoukalas et al. Whereas these conjectures had previously been attempted only with a bespoke agent, our open-source evaluation code runs any generic language model (LM) against them, and is secure against LM cheating attempts. We find that LMs equipped with a minimal set of tools resolve 147 of these conjectures with a budget of \$50 per attempt, scoring 30% on OEIS Open. OEIS Open Lite is a random subset of 100 conjectures for cheaper evaluation. When evaluated with a budget of \$200 per attempt, the best current LM scores 44% on OEIS Open Lite. Giving LMs access to the mathematics literature via 476,000 papers from arXiv did not increase performance on OEIS Open Lite, and nor did using more sophisticated agent loops. The conjectures covered in this work are of uncertain mathematical significance, and most have likely received little previous attention. Nevertheless, our results show that LMs can resolve open research conjectures autonomously and at modest cost.

</details>


### [194] [DCM Bandits: Multiplayer Information Asymmetric Cascading Bandits for Multiple Clicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11873)
*Andy Wang,Charlton Shih,William Chang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文首次将依赖点击模型（DCM）Bandits 扩展到多玩家信息不对称场景，提出三种至少含一种不对称性（动作或奖励）的设置，并给出亚线性遗憾保证；实验验证了算法有效性，并揭示反馈结构（全点击 vs 首次点击）对协调探索的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DCM Bandits 研究主要集中于单智能体，而现实搜索排序场景中多个用户（智能体）共享同一结果列表并可能产生多次点击，且各智能体在可选动作和获得的奖励上天然存在不对称性，现有算法无法直接适用，亟需新的选择策略。

Method: 通过将问题划分为三类不对称设置（仅动作不对称、仅奖励不对称、两者均不对称），分别为其设计基于 DCM 参数估计的探索-利用策略；针对小终止概率情形，利用反馈结构特性简化排名估计，并严格证明各设置的亚线性遗憾上界。

Result: 所有三类不对称设置均获得亚线性遗憾界，且在小终止概率下无需已知终止排名仍能达到良好性能，优于先前单智能体结果；实验表明算法在不同非对称环境下均表现稳健，且全点击反馈相比首次点击反馈能更高效地协调多智能体探索并降低累积遗憾。

Conclusion: 尽管未解决这些设置的信息论下界问题，但本文的工作为多智能体、非对称 DCM Bandits 建立了首个可用的算法框架，并强调反馈结构设计在分布式在线学习中的核心作用，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Dependent Click Model (DCM) Bandits to a multiplayer information-asymmetric setting, where multiple agents interact with a shared ranked list and may observe multiple clicks per session, introducing new challenges for selection strategies. We study asymmetry in (1) actions and (2) rewards, providing sublinear regret guarantees for three settings where at least one asymmetry is present. Establishing matching information-theoretic lower bounds for these settings is left as an open problem. We further show that for small termination probabilities, the termination ranking need not be known, improving on prior single-agent results. Experiments confirm that our algorithms perform well across asymmetric environments and highlight the critical role of feedback structure, specifically the distinction between full versus first-click feedback, in coordinating exploration and minimizing regret.

</details>


### [195] [ExRole: From Team Trajectories to Executable Roles in Multi-Agent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11949)
*Zhou Liu,Chaoyang Han,Zewei Pan,Zeli Su,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ExRole是一个将轨迹转化为可执行控制变量的角色学习框架，通过未来感知的原型学习、可读指令生成和LoRA槽位路由，在多跳问答基准上显著提升性能，并验证了角色行为的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多智能体系统将角色视为手工编写的提示标签，与学习行为和参数更新脱节。作者认为有用的角色应作为可执行的控制变量，既能总结预测未来效用的行为，又能指导后续交互并识别负责该行为的可训练能力。

Method: ExRole从局部团队轨迹中学习未来感知的角色原型，将其解析为可读指令和令牌对齐的角色标记，并可选地通过回合对齐的信用分配路由共享LoRA秩槽位。

Result: 在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上，ExRole相比单智能体搜索分别提升15.0/14.4和13.5/16.1的EM/F1；相比最强的非ExRole基线，增益仍达11.5/11.6和7.7/9.7。受控实验一致优于无角色、手动、随机和打乱角色；Role-Agent-Turn干预显示角色捕获了超越固定智能体身份或回合位置的可转移行为专门化。

Conclusion: 轨迹诱导的角色条件化是有效的，角色可作为可执行控制变量，且其行为专门化具有可迁移性，验证了ExRole框架在多智能体系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Roles provide an interpretable interface for organizing language-model agents, yet most multi-agent systems treat them as hand-written prompt labels disconnected from learned behavior and parameter updates. We argue that a useful role should instead be an executable control variable: it should summarize behavior predictive of future utility, guide subsequent interaction, and identify the trainable capacity responsible for that behavior. We introduce ExRole, a trajectory-to-role framework that learns future-aware role prototypes from prefix-local team traces, resolves them into readable instructions and token-aligned role markers, and optionally routes shared LoRA rank slots with turn-aligned credit. Across MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA, ExRole improves over single-agent search by 15.0/14.4 and 13.5/16.1 EM/F1 points, respectively. Against the strongest non-ExRole controls, the corresponding gains remain 11.5/11.6 and 7.7/9.7 points.
